    );

    // ********************
    // Pipeline 5: Output Reg
    // ********************
    `FFNR(result_o,enc_result,clk)
endmodule

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Compact posit decoder. Bit-identical function to posit_decoder_generic
// (including the zero/NaR quirks: runlength = n-1 when the magnitude is zero,
// implicit bit = |operand_i[n-2:0]), but built from a one-hot run-length decode
// instead of lzc + barrel shifter: much smaller and shallower.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
module posit_decoder #(
    parameter int unsigned n = 8,
    parameter int unsigned es = 2,
    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),
    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,
    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3
)(
    input  logic [n-1:0] operand_i,
    output logic sign_o,
    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,
    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o
);
    localparam int unsigned W = n-1;
    localparam int unsigned MF_W = W-es-2;      // kept fraction bits

    assign sign_o = operand_i[n-1];

    logic [W-1:0] mag_neg;
    prefix_add #(
        .WIDTH(W)
    ) u_neg (
        .a(~operand_i[W-1:0]),
        .b({W{1'b0}}),
        .cin(1'b1),
        .sum(mag_neg),
        .cout()
    );
    logic [W-1:0] value;
    assign value = sign_o ? mag_neg : operand_i[W-1:0];
    wire regS = value[W-1];

    // one-hot run length: sel[r-1] == 1  <=>  regime run length r (r = 1..W)
    //   regS=1: run of leading ones;  regS=0: run of leading zeros (value==0 -> r=W)
    logic [W-1:0] sel;
    generate
        genvar r, i;
        for (r = 1; r < W; r++) begin : g_sel
            assign sel[r-1] = regS ? ((&value[W-1:W-r]) & ~value[W-1-r]) :
                                     ((~|value[W-1:W-r]) &  value[W-1-r]);
        end
    endgenerate
    assign sel[W-1] = regS ? &value : ~|value;

    // per-run field options: rg_exp = {k, e} with k = regS ? r-1 : -r and
    // e / mf taken from value at the run-dependent positions
    // (equivalent to op_no_rg = value << (r+1), e = op_no_rg[W-1:W-es],
    //  mf = op_no_rg[W-1-es:2])
    logic [(EXP_WIDTH+1)*W-1:0] rgexp_opts;
    logic [MF_W*W-1:0]          mf_opts;
    generate
        for (r = 1; r <= W; r++) begin : g_opts
            for (i = 0; i < es; i++) begin : g_exp
                if (W-es+i-r-1 >= 0) begin : g_e1
                    assign rgexp_opts[(r-1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + i] = value[W-es+i-r-1];
                end
                else begin : g_e0
                    assign rgexp_opts[(r-1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + i] = 1'b0;
                end
            end
            wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] k_part = regS ? ((r-1) << es) : ((-r) << es);
            assign rgexp_opts[(r-1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + es +: (nd+1)] = k_part[EXP_WIDTH:es];
            for (i = 0; i < MF_W; i++) begin : g_mf
                if (i+1-r >= 0) begin : g_m1
                    assign mf_opts[(r-1)*MF_W + i] = value[i+1-r];
                end
                else begin : g_m0
                    assign mf_opts[(r-1)*MF_W + i] = 1'b0;
                end
            end
        end
    endgenerate

    // one-hot OR-mux
    logic [MF_W-1:0] mf_sel;
    always_comb begin
        rg_exp_o = '0;
        mf_sel = '0;
        for (int rr = 0; rr < W; rr++) begin
            rg_exp_o = rg_exp_o | (rgexp_opts[rr*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] & {(EXP_WIDTH+1){sel[rr]}});
            mf_sel  = mf_sel  | (mf_opts[rr*MF_W +: MF_W] & {MF_W{sel[rr]}});
        end

... (showing lines 1790-1884 of 1887; use offset/limit for more)

((r-1) << es) : ((-r) << es);\n            assign rgexp_opts[(r-1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + es +: (nd+1)] = k_part[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n            for (i = 0; i < MF_W; i++) begin : g_mf\n                if (r <= (i+1)) begin : g_m1", old_string="            for (i = 0; i < es; i++) begin : g_exp\n                if (W-es+i-r-1 >= 0) begin : g_e1\n                    assign rgexp_opts[(r-1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + i] = value[W-es+i-r-1];\n                end\n                else begin : g_e0\n                    assign rgexp_opts[(r-1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + i] = 1'b0;\n                end\n            end\n            wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] k_part = regS ? Key insight: the shift-clamp sits on cloud B's critical path, but it's **unnecessary** — shifting a 14-bit value by ≥14 already yields 0, so I only need to kill terms with diff≥16 (computable in parallel) instead of muxing the shift amount:

The kill flag is computed in parallel\n            // with the low shift bits and applied after the barrel shifter, so\n            // no clamp logic sits on the shift-amount path.\n            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin: diff_kill\n                assign kill_amount[z] = |rg_exp_diff[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)];\n            end\n            else begin: no_kill\n                assign kill_amount[z] = 1'b0;\n            end\n            assign shift_amount[(z)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = rg_exp_diff[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: SHIFT_WIDTH];\n        end\n    endgenerate", old_string="    logic [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_diff;\n    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    generate\n        genvar z;\n        for(z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin: align_amount\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_item_z = rg_exp_items[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n            prefix_add #(\n                .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH+1)\n            ) u_diff_add(\n                .a(rg_exp_max),\n                .b(~exp_item_z),\n                .cin(1'b1),\n                .sum(rg_exp_diff[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .cout()\n            );\n            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin: diff_clamp\n                assign shift_amount[(z)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = (|rg_exp_diff[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? [ASSISTANT]
**Critical discovery**: 300 extra flops changed area by only **+0.5 units** — flops are essentially *free*; area is ~100% combinational logic! This means I can use wide pipeline state to rebalance the combinational clouds freely. Current clouds: ~27|33|33|26|25 cells; the ideal 6-way balance of the ~130-cell chain is ~22-24 cells each (~830ps vs 1106ps).

I'll write a v2 top with re-balanced cuts (decode | expadd+max | diff+shift+CSA½ | CSA½+add+abs | normalize | encode) plus a splittable Dadda 3:2 reduction tree:

## Summary

I optimized the 5-stage pipelined posit dot-product-accumulate unit (`pdpu_top_pipelined` in `starting_point.v`), improving the area-delay product from the starting point's **968,045 → 754,752** (-22%), while keeping bit-exact function and the 5-cycle latency.

**Key optimizations, in order of impact:**

1. **Pipeline re-balancing with free flops** (920→850ps): A probe showed register flops cost essentially nothing (300 flops = +0.5 area units) — the area is ~100% combinational logic. This allowed rebalancing the pipeline cuts purely for depth: carry-save exponent adds before pipe1, the max-tournament split across pipe2/pipe3, CSA reduction split across pipe3/pipe4, and normalize/encode split across pipe4/pipe5, keeping every combinational cloud ≈20 gate levels.

2. **Offset-binary unsigned max tree** (839→790ps, new best): Exponents are XORed with 0x40 so the comparator tree is a pure unsigned max (the sign-handling logic disappears); exponent differences are invariant under the offset.

3. **Algorithmic equivalents**: plain `*` instead of the booth/CSA multiplier (sum+carry = exact product); sign-folding (invert terms + one popcount operand into the CSA tree instead of 17 × 20-bit incrementers); kill-flag instead of shift clamping (shifting 14 bits by ≥14 already yields 0); fused round+negate in the encoder (`sign ? -(v+r) : (v+r) == (sign ? ~v : v) + (r^sign)`); fused final add + absolute value (`-(a+b) = ~a + ~b + 2` in parallel with `a+b`); Sklansky parallel-prefix adders where ripple chains dominated.

4. **Compact posit decoder** (run-length one-hot decode) and a Dadda 3:2 reduction tree for the 18-term accumulation.

All variants were evaluated after every change; the best-passing design (eval 21) is kept.

## Final Results

| Metric | Starting point | Final | Improvement |
|---|---|---|---|
| Area-delay product | 968,045 | **754,752** | **-22%** |
| Delay | 910 ps | 790 ps | -13% |
| Area | 1063.5 | 954.9 | -10% |

**What was done** (all transformations bit-exact vs. the combinational oracle):

1. **Micro-architectural equivalents**: `*` replaces the booth/CSA multiplier (downstream only used sum+carry = exact product); sign-folding (invert terms + popcount operand instead of 17 × 20-bit incrementers); kill-flag instead of shift-clamp mux; fused round+negate (`sign ? ~(v+r)+1 : v+r` → one adder); fused final-add + abs (`-(a+b) = ~a+~b+2` computed in parallel with `a+b`).

2. **Free-flop rebalancing** (key insight from a flop-area probe: registers are essentially free — 300 flops = +0.5 area units): wide pipeline state lets each of the 6 combinational clouds be cut at ~20 gate levels — carry-save exponent adds before pipe1, exponent max-tournament split 4/1 across pipe2/pipe3, Dadda 3:2 reduction split across pipe3/pipe4, normalize/encode split across pipe4/pipe5.

3. **Offset-binary max tree**: XOR-ing exponents with 0x40 turns the signed comparator tree into a pure unsigned max (differences are offset-invariant), removing the sign-handling logic from the critical path.

4. **Sklansky parallel-prefix adders** where yosys would infer ripple chains, plus a compact run-length posit decoder and a splittable Dadda reduction tree.

The critical path (exponent add + 4-level max tournament, 790ps) and all other clouds (~750-790ps) are now balanced at the structural floor for this function.

## Summary

**Final result**: `starting_point.v` — PASS on all 1,999,992 vectors, ADP **754,752** (from baseline 968,045, −22%; delay 910→790 ps, area 1063→955).

### 1. Approaches tried and what worked
- **Baseline first**: evaluated `starting_point.v` untouched (968k), then made incremental, re-evaluated changes.
- **Equivalence-preserving datapath rewrites**: `*` for the 4×4 booth/CSA multiplier (sum+carry was the exact product); sign-folding (invert terms + one popcount CSA operand replaces 17×20-bit incrementers); kill-flag replacing the shift-clamp mux (shifting 14 bits ≥14 already yields 0); fused encoder round+negate (`sign ? -(v+r) : (v+r) == (sign?~v:v)+(r^sign)`); fused final-add + abs (`-(a+b) = ~a+~b+2` in parallel with `a+b`).
- **Pipeline re-balancing** (biggest win): a probe showed flops are essentially free (300 flops = +0.5 area units), so wide pipeline state was used purely for depth balance — carry-save exponent adds before pipe1, max-tournament split 4/1 across pipe2/pipe3, Dadda 3:2 reduction split across pipe3/pipe4, normalize/encode split across pipe4/pipe5. Every cloud ended at ~20 gate levels.
- **Offset-binary max tree** (final big win): XOR exponents with 0x40 so the comparator is pure unsigned max (differences are offset-invariant) — removed sign logic from the critical path (839→790 ps).
- **Structural adders**: Sklansky `prefix_add` where yosys inferred ripple chains; Dadda 3:2 `csa_red` (splittable) for the 18-term accumulation.

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. Pipeline re-balancing enabled by the free-flop discovery (−15% delay).
2. Offset-binary unsigned max tree (−6% delay).
3. Multiplier replacement + sign folding (−19% area at the start).
4. Fused add/abs and round/negate (delay-neutral area wins).

### 3. Regressions / dead ends
- **prefix_add bug**: `sum = pt ^ {go, cin}` is wrong for `cin=1` — needed `carry_in[j] = go[j-1] | (ppo[j-1] & cin)`; caused mass failures until fixed.
- **Unsigned comparison bug** in the compact decoder: `if (W-es+i-r-1 >= 0)` with `int unsigned` params is always true → out-of-range selects corrupted exponent bits (mantissas unaffected). Fixed with wrap-safe `(r+1) <= (W-es+i)`.
- **Off-by-one sticky** in the encoder funnel reformulation (`<= 2` vs `< 2`) → 339 rounding errors.
- **Regressions vs. best**: compact OR-mux decoder (area-neutral, no gain), diff via plain `-` vs prefix (757k vs 754k), max-tree regrouping (813k), CSA_MID=8 (803k), encoder funnel shift (769k), target_delay 850/900 (833k/858k). Reverted all; the flow is deterministic so the best config (`CSA_MID=6`, barrel-shifter encoder, `(5,5,5,2)`+pair max grouping) is restored and re-verified.

### 4. Lessons learned
- **Measure area composition before optimizing**: the flop probe revealed flops ≈ 0 area — this completely changed the optimal strategy (wide pipeline state is free). A second probe showed the 32 posit decoders cost only 9 area units, killing a planned decoder rewrite.
- **Balance all clouds, not just the worst one**: shaving one cloud just moved the critical path elsewhere; the win came from re-cutting the whole pipeline.
- Exploit arithmetic identities (offset binary, invert+popcount, `-(a+b)=~a+~b+2`) — they are provably bit-exact and remove real logic.
- Watch Verilog signedness/width pitfalls (`int unsigned` in genvar expressions, macro args with commas) — they fail silently at RTL.
- Small RTL variants move the mapper ±4%; only structural changes exceed noise, so re-verify and keep the best measurement.
